# Day 04 — 2026-09-01

Zone 6 · 3,109 arc traversals · 6.17 h driving + 0.75 h break = **6.92 h**

Capture 1.62 h · deadhead 2.18 h · turns 2.37 h


Start and finish at the depot. **Capture** rows are required footage: keep to 40 km/h or below unless the row says *reduced*. **Deadhead** rows are repositioning — drive them normally, the camera is not counted.


> **6 arcs on this route are marked [UNVERIFIED].** They come from the customer's street layer and not from OpenStreetMap, so their direction, width and junction rules are assumptions, not tags. Treat them as two-way residential, obey what the signs actually say, and note any one-way, gate or building site you find — see `surveys/unverified-streets.md`.
